However, direct measurement of inference energy often requires hardware telemetry, power instrumentation, or infrastructure-specific monitoring, limiting its applicability in comparative studies, early-stage system design, and sustainability reporting. This report presents an analytically structured, empirically calibrated, GPU-level methodology for estimating LLM inference energy on NVIDIA H100-class accelerators without direct runtime measurement. The proposed estimator combines parameter-scaled transformer FLOP accounting, calibrated memory-traffic factors, and hardware-specific energy coefficients for FP16/BF16 tensor-core computation and high-bandwidth-memory movement. It explicitly separates prompt prefill from autoregressive decoding, enabling energy estimates for input tokens, output tokens, and complete inference requests. The methodology further decomposes total energy into compute, parameter-access, key-value-cache write, and attention-read components, allowing the scaling behavior with model size, context length, and generated-token count to be analyzed. The resulting estimates are not intended to replace physical power measurements; rather, they provide transparent, reproducible, and assumption-explicit approximations suitable for model comparison, green-coding analysis, and design-time evaluation of LLM inference workloads.

</details>


### [234] [Simultaneous Coverage and Efficiency Guarantee in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26577)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自适应共形推断（ACI）提出统一在线学习框架，同时解决三个核心缺陷：绝对覆盖控制（非抵消性）、预测集效率、以及动态基准下的效率保证。在完全对抗、随机全反馈、协变量依赖随机三种设定下分别给出算法及理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs和Candès提出的ACI及其变体是分布漂移下在线共形预测的标准方法，但存在三个根本缺陷：（1）只控制有符号的长期覆盖误差，允许正负误差相互抵消，导致方法可能在长时间内严重偏误却仍满足理论保证；（2）现有保证对预测集大小毫无约束，可通过无限扩大预测集来满足覆盖要求；（3）已有效率保证以事后选择的固定预测器为基准，一旦数据分布漂移，最优阈值本身随时间变化，固定基准意义骤减。

Method: 作者提出统一在线学习框架，在三种设定下分别处理：（1）完全对抗设定：利用ACI更新等价于pinball损失上的投影在线梯度下降，对任意单调Lipschitz效率目标，在没有分布或凸性假设下推导出同时的覆盖与效率保证；（2）随机全反馈设定：提出滑动窗口分位数追踪器，并建立匹配的minimax下界证明算法速率最优；（3）协变量依赖的随机设定：开发分区ACI算法，追踪函数值形式的oracle阈值，给出同时覆盖与效率保证。

Result: 在完全对抗设定下，对任意单调Lipschitz效率目标实现了同时的绝对覆盖与效率保证；在随机全反馈设定下，滑动窗口分位数追踪器被证明达到minimax速率最优；在协变量依赖随机设定下，分区ACI算法能追踪函数值oracle阈值并实现双重保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一在线学习框架有效解决了ACI的三个根本缺陷，在三种重要模型设定下均能同时控制绝对覆盖违规和预测集效率，并相对于动态演化的基准提供理论保证，显著推进了分布漂移下在线共形预测的理论基础。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal inference (ACI) of Gibbs and Cand{è}s and its variants are the standard approach to online conformal prediction under distribution shift, but they suffer from three fundamental limitations. First, their guarantees control only the \emph{signed} long-run coverage error: persistent miscoverage in one direction can be masked by compensating errors later, so a method can satisfy the theoretical guarantee while being badly wrong for extended periods. Second, existing guarantees say nothing about prediction-set size, so validity can be achieved trivially at the cost of unduly wide prediction sets. Third, the efficiency guarantees that do exist compare against a \emph{fixed} predictor chosen in hindsight, a benchmark that becomes increasingly less meaningful once the data-generating distribution shifts, since the very notion of an optimal threshold then changes over time.
  We consider a unified online learning framework that simultaneously controls absolute, non-cancelling coverage violation and prediction-set efficiency against a dynamically evolving benchmark for three important models. In the fully adversarial setting, exploiting the fact that the standard ACI update is exactly projected online gradient descent on the pinball loss, we derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees for arbitrary monotone Lipschitz efficiency objectives, with no distributional or {\it convexity} assumptions. In the stochastic setting with full-score feedback, we propose a sliding-window quantile tracker and establish a matching minimax lower bound showing our algorithm is rate-optimal. In the covariate-dependent stochastic setting, we develop a partitioned ACI algorithm that tracks a function-valued oracle threshold, and derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees.

</details>


### [235] [Benchmarking ConvLSTM for One-Day-Ahead IMDAA Rainfall-Field Prediction across Four Indian Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26581)
*Tanmay Ghosh,Shaurabh Anand,Rakesh Gomaji Nannewar,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究测试了ConvLSTM在基于日再分析网格的一日降水预报中的表现，发现其并未一致优于FC-LSTM、持续性预报等更简单的基线方法。ConvLSTM仅在孟买的空间异常预报中略有优势，且与FC-LSTM差距很小。神经网络模型普遍低估降雨量级，对强降雨日的检测能力不及持续性预报。研究表明，网格化输入本身并不足以构成选用ConvLSTM的理由，架构选择应基于全面的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ConvLSTM广泛用于降水预报，但其性能证据主要来自密集的高频雷达序列。作者质疑：在稀疏的日尺度再分析网格上，卷积循环结构是否真的能改善一日超前降雨场预报？现有文献缺乏在低时空分辨率网格数据上的系统性对比。

Method: 使用IMDAA（印度季风数据同化与分析）1998-2020年6-9月的再分析数据，针对班加罗尔、德里、加尔各答和孟买四个城市。比较了10种方法（朴素方法、统计方法、树模型和神经网络），在两种输入配置下（仅大气变量 vs 降雨历史+大气变量）进行评估。评估维度涵盖完整降雨场误差、区域平均降雨量、空间异常和强降雨日检测能力，并进行事后敏感性分析。

Result: FC-LSTM在班加罗尔、加尔各答和孟买的区域平均降雨误差最低，持续性预报在德里表现最好。ConvLSTM仅在孟买的空间异常误差上数值最低，该地降雨场具有更强的短期空间连续性。然而ConvLSTM与FC-LSTM的差异很小。所有神经网络模型均低估降雨量级，在强降雨日预测的阈值超限数过少，而持续性预报在所有城市的检测性能均最优。事后分析显示，所选模型对最新输入日最敏感，孟买的模型对更广泛的历史滞后也表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: 网格化输入本身不足以构成选择ConvLSTM的理由。在实际应用中，应在平均性能、空间性能和强降雨性能三个维度上进行严格的基准测试后再决定架构选择，简单模型（如持续性预报和FC-LSTM）往往已具备竞争力。

Abstract: Convolutional long short-term memory networks (ConvLSTMs) are widely used for precipitation forecasting, but most evidence for their performance comes from dense, high-frequency radar sequences. This study tests whether convolutional recurrence improves one-day-ahead rainfall-field prediction on small daily reanalysis grids. Indian Monsoon Data Assimilation and Analysis (IMDAA) fields for June-September 1998-2020 were analysed for Bengaluru, Delhi, Kolkata and Mumbai. Ten naive, statistical, tree-based and neural approaches were compared using atmospheric-only and rainfall-history-plus-atmospheric inputs. Performance was assessed for complete fields, domain-mean rainfall, spatial anomalies and high-rainfall days.
  ConvLSTM did not consistently outperform simpler alternatives. FC-LSTM produced the numerically lowest domain-mean rainfall error in Bengaluru, Kolkata and Mumbai, whereas persistence performed best in Delhi. ConvLSTM produced the numerically lowest spatial-anomaly error only in Mumbai, where rainfall fields showed greater short-term spatial continuity and rainfall-history inputs improved all three neural architectures. The difference between ConvLSTM and FC-LSTM was nevertheless small. Neural models underestimated rainfall magnitude and predicted too few threshold exceedances on high-rainfall days, while persistence achieved the highest detection performance in every city. Post-hoc analyses showed that the selected models were most sensitive to the latest input day, with broader recent-lag sensitivity in Mumbai. These findings show that gridded inputs alone do not justify ConvLSTM and that architecture choice should follow strong benchmarking across average, spatial and high-rainfall performance.

</details>


### [236] [Uncertainty-Guided LLM Semantic Augmentation for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26599)
*Jialu Xu,Mengkun Liang,Guannan Liu,Xiaojie Mao,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CURL（因果不确定性引导的表示学习），一种即插即用的适配器，利用估计器的不确定性将预训练语义容量分配给局部不稳定的单元。CURL通过双角色提示查询冻结的大语言模型，构建分配导向和异质性导向的表示，并通过分离路径进行路由，在多个基准上提升了十种宿主学习器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限样本下估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）需要同时学习协变量调整的滋扰结构、处理效应异质性以及X的有效表示。原始数值和类别编码会丢失语义关系和高阶交互，导致联合任务局部不稳定。一项激励性研究进一步表明，这种不稳定性通过可部分分离的分配侧和异质性侧通道显现。

Method: 提出CURL（Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning），一种即插即用适配器。其核心机制包括：(1) 使用估计器不确定性来引导预训练语义容量向局部不稳定单元的分配；(2) 通过两个角色条件化提示查询冻结的大语言模型；(3) 从观测协变量中分别构建分配导向和异质性导向的表示；(4) 通过分离的路径对两类表示进行路由。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，CURL在大多数设置中提升了十种宿主学习器的性能。消融实验、精细化动态分析、路由重分配分析以及探针分析均支持所设计的架构及双通道的预期角色。

Conclusion: CURL通过引入LLM驱动的语义表示和不确定性引导的双通道路由机制，有效缓解了CATE估计中的局部不稳定性问题，展现出对多种宿主学习器的广泛兼容性和稳健的性能提升。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects is central to targeted interventions, such as personalized promotions and precision medicine. We focus on the conditional average treatment effect (CATE), a standard estimand for characterizing such heterogeneity. Even under standard identification conditions, finite-sample CATE estimation requires learning the nuisance structure for covariate adjustment and treatment-effect heterogeneity, often together with an effective representation of X. Raw numerical and categorical encodings can leave semantic relations and higher-order interactions implicit, making this joint task locally unstable. A motivating study further shows that this instability appears through partially separable assignment- and heterogeneity-side channels. Building on this observation, we propose CURL (Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning), a plug-in adapter that uses estimator uncertainty to allocate pretrained semantic capacity to locally unstable units. CURL queries a frozen LLM through two role-conditioned prompts, constructs assignment- and heterogeneity-oriented representations from the observed covariates, and routes them through separated pathways. On four benchmarks, CURL improves ten host learners in most settings, while ablation, refinement-dynamics, route-reassignment, and probe analyses support the intended design and roles of the two channels.

</details>


### [237] [FedWeave: Rethinking the Unit of Specialization in Heterogeneous Federated MoE-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26618)
*Donghang Duan,Xu Zheng,Lizong Zhang,Chong Mu,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedWeave 提出非对称聚合框架，通过原型级专家聚合与混合任务路由器训练，解决联邦 PEFT 中的任务异构性问题，实现稀疏推理下接近软路由的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦参数高效微调（Federated PEFT）中，客户端间任务异构性导致跨任务干扰和梯度冲突。现有联邦 MoE-LoRA 方法按客户端粒度进行专家特化，隐含假设客户端内任务一致，无法处理客户端内也存在任务混合的场景。核心洞察：专家需要“纯净”（模式一致的更新以保持特化），而路由器需要“对比”（混合任务观测以支持专家比较）。

Method: 提出 FedWeave 框架，采用非对称聚合策略：（1）专家聚合与路由器优化分离；（2）通过无监督原型发现（prototype discovery）形成本地桶（buckets），并在客户端间对齐原型，实现原型级专家聚合；（3）保留混合任务的客户端轨迹用于路由器训练。推理时采用稀疏推理，仅激活一个专家，同时几乎保留全部软路由性能。理论上分析了非对称聚合的优势：控制专家收敛的平稳性、识别路由器轨迹碎片化导致的共识误差、界定稀疏推理风险。

Result: 在异构多任务基准测试上，使用主流 LLM 骨干网络，FedWeave 一致优于强基线方法；消融实验验证了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: FedWeave 通过非对称聚合有效协调了联邦 MoE 中专家纯度与路由器对比性的矛盾需求，为联邦 PEFT 场景下的任务异构性问题提供了理论支撑和实践验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated PEFT enables LLMs to collaboratively adapt to decentralized private data without sharing raw examples. However, task heterogeneity across clients can cause cross-task interference and gradient conflicts during aggregation. Federated MoE-LoRA addresses this challenge through specialized LoRA experts and conditional routing. Yet existing methods typically specialize at client granularity, implicitly assuming task-coherent clients. Our core insight is that experts need purity, namely pattern-coherent updates that preserve specialization, whereas routers need contrast, namely mixed-task observations that support expert comparison. We propose FedWeave, a framework that adopts asymmetric aggregation, separating expert aggregation from router optimization to meet these two requirements. FedWeave uses unsupervised prototype discovery to form local buckets and align them across clients, enabling prototype-level expert aggregation while retaining mixed-task client trajectories for router training. At inference, FedWeave performs sparse inference with one active expert while preserving nearly all soft-routing performance. Our theoretical analysis explains why asymmetric aggregation is advantageous: it controls expert convergence in stationarity through off-pattern contamination, identifies the consensus error induced by fragmented router trajectories, and bounds sparse-inference risk. On a heterogeneous multi-task benchmark with mainstream LLM backbones, FedWeave consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablations verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [238] [Enhancing Automated Machine Learning via Homogeneous Train-Test Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26625)
*Yearn Tan Yin Tze,Charles Grellois*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练-测试集划分中统计相似性的作用，比较了五种划分策略在15个UCI数据集上的表现。结果表明，基于几何的方法（Kennard-Stone、Duplex、SPXY）虽然MMD分数趋近于零，但会引入性能估计不稳定性。所提出的Optimised-Distribution方法将相似性作为显式优化目标，平均MMD相似度达到89.0%，优于所有对比策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准随机划分假设训练集和测试集具有相同的底层分布，但在类别不平衡、自然聚类或空间自相关的数据集中，该假设常常被违反。现有划分策略未将统计相似性作为核心考量，可能导致AutoML模型评估偏差，因此需要系统研究统计相似性对数据划分及模型评估的影响。

Method: 在15个UCI基准数据集上对比五种划分策略：随机划分、分层抽样、Kennard-Stone、Duplex和SPXY。采用卡方检验、Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验和最大均值差异（MMD）三种统计检验评估训练-测试集相似性。提出Optimised-Distribution方法，将分布相似性作为显式优化目标进行数据划分。

Result: 基于几何的方法（Kennard-Stone、Duplex、SPXY）始终产生接近零的MMD分数，但会引入下游性能估计的不稳定性。所提出的Optimised-Distribution方法在所有评估策略中实现了最高的平均MMD相似度，达到89.0%。

Conclusion: 统计相似性在训练-测试集划分中具有关键作用，不应被忽视。将分布相似性作为显式优化目标的Optimised-Distribution方法在保持高相似度的同时避免了几何方法的不稳定性问题，为AutoML模型评估提供了更可靠的划分策略。

Abstract: Accurate model evaluation in machine learning depends critically on how datasets are split into training and testing subsets. Standard random splitting assumes that both partitions share the same underlying distribution, an assumption often violated in datasets with class imbalance, natural clustering, or spatial autocorrelation. This paper investigates the role of statistical similarity in train-test splitting and its consequences for AutoML model evaluation. Five established strategies are compared across fifteen UCI benchmark datasets: random splitting, stratified sampling, Kennard-Stone, Duplex, and SPXY. Similarity is assessed using chi-square, Kolmogorov-Smirnov, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) tests. Geometry-based methods consistently produce near-zero MMD scores, introducing instability into downstream performance estimates. The proposed Optimised-Distribution method treats similarity as an explicit optimisation objective and achieves the highest mean MMD similarity, 89.0%, across all strategies evaluated.

</details>


### [239] [Understanding Context Sampling in TabPFN on Small Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26628)
*Mohammed Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了TabPFN中上下文选择的三个核心问题：上下文大小对预测稳定性的影响、准确率的关键驱动因素（分布代表性 vs. 特征空间覆盖），以及昂贵的选择方法（K-Means、最远点采样）相比随机采样的收益。研究发现：更大上下文同时提升准确率与稳定性；特征空间的多样性和覆盖度（而非分布均值匹配）是准确率的更强预测因子；随机采样因其期望上的特征空间覆盖而表现出色，而昂贵的选择方法仅带来边际增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN通过上下文学习进行分类，但在小规模表格数据集上，用户仍需手动选择上下文大小及构成。这些选择对预测稳定性、准确率和计算成本的实际影响尚未被系统研究，这促使作者探究上下文设计选择如何影响模型表现，以及是否存在简单有效的启发式方法。

Method: 在15个OpenML数据集上进行重复上下文采样实验：(1) 改变上下文大小k（16到数千），测量AUC变异系数评估稳定性；(2) 设计对照实验，分离分布代表性（特征均值匹配）与特征空间覆盖度对准确率的影响；(3) 使用混合效应模型量化多样性、覆盖度和特征均值偏移对准确率的相对贡献；(4) 对比K-Means、最远点采样与均匀随机采样的准确率与选择成本。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 更大上下文稳定且准确——k=16时AUC变异系数约6-18%，大上下文降至1-4%；(2) 强制匹配特征均值反而使准确率下降最多0.5 AUC，因其降低了上下文多样性；(3) 混合效应分析显示，多样性（beta=+0.23, p=3×10⁻¹²）和覆盖度是准确率的显著预测因子，而特征均值偏移（beta=-0.01, p=0.71）不显著；(4) K-Means和最远点采样与随机采样准确率相当，但选择成本高出2-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 随机采样成功的原因在于它在期望意义上提供了特征空间覆盖，而非因为它再现了底层数据分布。因此，在实践中，使用尽可能大的随机采样上下文是简单且高效的选择策略，无需采用昂贵的主动选择方法。

Abstract: TabPFN performs classification through in-context learning: it conditions on a set of labeled training rows (the context, or prototypes) and predicts test labels without gradient updates. On small tabular datasets, practitioners must still choose the context size and which rows constitute the context. We study how these choices affect prediction stability, accuracy, and selection cost using repeated context sampling on 15 OpenML datasets. Specifically, we investigate (i) whether larger contexts reduce prediction variability across random draws, (ii) whether accuracy depends on preserving the training distribution or on feature-space coverage, and (iii) whether expensive selection methods such as K-Means and farthest-point sampling provide benefits over uniform random sampling.
  We find that larger contexts are both more accurate and substantially more stable, with AUC coefficient of variation decreasing from roughly 6 to 18% at k=16 to 1 to 4% at larger context sizes on datasets with room for improvement. Although accuracy correlates with distribution representativeness in random contexts, controlled experiments show that matching feature means alone can reduce accuracy by up to 0.5 AUC because it reduces context diversity. Mixed-effects analysis identifies diversity and coverage, rather than feature-mean matching, as the stronger predictor of accuracy (diversity beta=+0.23, p=3x10^-12; feature-mean shift beta=-0.01, p=0.71). K-Means and farthest-point sampling achieve similar accuracy to random selection while requiring two to three orders of magnitude more selection cost. These results show that random sampling succeeds because it provides feature-space coverage in expectation, not because it reproduces the underlying data distribution.

</details>


### [240] [RAG-HAR+: Towards Cost-Efficient LLM-Based Human Activity Recognition for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26631)
*Hansi Karunarathna,Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Chamara Madarasingha,Anura Jayasumana,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAG-HAR+ 是一种免训练、检索优先、成本优化的 HAR 框架：通过离线检索设计器智能体生成数据集特定的特征组来增强检索质量，仅在检索证据不足时才调用 LLM 进行歧义消解，在六个基准数据集上保持竞争力性能的同时显著降低了 LLM 使用量和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法需要针对特定数据集训练、大量标注数据，且更换传感器设置或活动分类体系时需反复适配。RAG-HAR 虽提出免训练的检索增强生成方案，但对 LLM 推理依赖过重，导致成本高、延迟大。

Method: 1) 离线检索设计器智能体从丰富的运动描述符池中自动设计数据集特定的特征组，使传感器窗口比较更贴合数据集活动模式；2) 推理阶段采用 k 近邻多数投票：检索证据强时直接分类，仅对不确定样本递交给 LLM 歧义消解器智能体处理。

Result: 在六个 HAR 基准数据集上，RAG-HAR+ 保持了与原有方法相当或更优的性能，同时显著降低了 LLM 调用次数、token 消耗和推理时间；移动端原型验证了该方案在实际场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 检索优先策略配合 LLM 辅助的架构，在保持 HAR 精度的同时有效降低了运营成本，为可穿戴传感器领域的免训练活动识别提供了更具实用性的技术路径。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors supports applications in healthcare, rehabilitation, fitness tracking, and smart environments. Yet, existing deep learning approaches require dataset-specific training, large labeled corpora, and repeated adaptation to new sensor settings or activity taxonomies. Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Human Activity Recognition (RAG-HAR) addresses this by framing HAR as a training-free, retrieval-augmented task, in which statistical descriptions of sensor windows are used to retrieve similar labeled examples that guide LLM-based classification. We introduce RAG-HAR+, a retrieval-first and cost-optimized extension that strengthens retrieval while reducing dependence on LLM-based inference. RAG-HAR+ uses an offline Retrieval Designer Agent to design dataset-specific feature groups from a diverse pool of motion descriptors, enabling sensor windows to be compared using features better aligned with dataset-specific activity patterns. During inference, RAG-HAR+ uses majority voting over retrieved neighbors for samples with strong retrieval evidence and defers only uncertain cases to an LLM-based Ambiguity Resolver Agent. Across six HAR benchmarks, RAG-HAR+ maintains competitive or improved performance while reducing LLM usage, token consumption, and inference time. We further extend the RAG-HAR mobile prototype to demonstrate the practical feasibility of retrieval-first, LLM-assisted HAR in mobile sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [241] [AIGen: Automating AI Bill of Materials Generation Through Hybrid MLOps Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26652)
*Federica Pepe,Daniele Bifolco,Costantino Martignetti,Aureliano D'Amici,Fabiano Izzo,Damian A. Tamburri,Massimiliano Di Penta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIGen 是一个基于 MLflow 的模块化 AI 物料清单生成工具，能够自动生成符合 SPDX 3.0 AI 配置文件标准的机器可读 AIBoM，通过挖掘启发式方法与 LLM 相结合，并支持插件扩展以适配 Hugging Face、PyTorch、TensorFlow 等异构 AI 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负责任的 AI 系统开发与部署需要对数据集、模型权重、训练流水线和运行时依赖等组件进行严格文档化。SPDX 3.0 虽已原生支持 AI 与数据集配置文件，但缺乏能够在自动化、可扩展方式下生成标准合规 AIBoM 的实用工具。

Method: AIGen 构建于 MLflow MLOps 框架之上，采用模块化架构：结合挖掘启发式规则与大型语言模型（LLM）来生成 AIBoM；提供插件接口，允许从业者在不修改核心代码的前提下以领域特定采集器扩展工具能力，从而支持 Hugging Face、PyTorch、TensorFlow 等多样化 AI 框架。

Result: 论文提出了 AIGen 工具，能够生成符合 SPDX 3.0 AI 配置文件的机器可读、可互操作的 AI 系统组件清单，并提供了工具的开源实现及演示视频。

Conclusion: AIGen 为透明、可问责的 AI 供应链治理提供了具体且可复用的基础，旨在促进符合欧盟 AI 法案、NIST AI 风险管理框架和 ISO/IEC 42001 等法规与标准的合规工作。

Abstract: The responsible development and deployment of artificial intelligence (AI) systems requires rigorous documentation of their constituent artifacts, e.g., datasets, model weights, training pipelines, and runtime dependencies. Although the Software Package Data Exchange (SPDX) 3.0 standard introduced native support for AI and dataset profiles, practical tooling capable of generating standards-compliant AI Bills of Materials (AIBoMs) in an automated and extensible manner remains scarce. This paper presents AIGen, a modular AIBoM generator that produces machine-readable, interoperable inventories of AI system components that comply with the SPDX 3.0 AI profile. AIGen works on top of the MLflow MLOps framework and combines mining heuristics with Large Language Models to generate AIBoMs. A plugin interface allows practitioners to extend the tool with domain-specific collectors without modifying the core codebase, supporting heterogeneous AI frameworks such as Hugging Face, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. AIGen is designed to facilitate compliance with the European Union AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework, and ISO/IEC 42001, providing a concrete, reusable foundation for transparent, accountable AI supply chain governance. Tool URL: https://github.com/danielebifolco/AIGen Tool Video: https://youtu.be/\_nAbXDWfVL4

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization for Risk-Aware AutoRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26680)
*Mingxuan Che,Tsung-Yuan Tseng,Theresa Eimer,Marius Lindauer,Alexander von Rohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERAHBO 是一种面向强化学习的风险厌恶异方差贝叶斯优化方法，通过同时对均值和方差建模，并结合自适应重采样策略，在保证高平均回报的同时降低训练运行的变异性，并在样本效率上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的训练结果具有高度随机性，且期望性能和方差均依赖于超参数配置。现有超参数优化方法要么忽视风险（方差），要么采用固定的重采样预算，导致样本效率低下。因此需要一种能同时优化回报均值与方差、且高效利用样本的超参数优化方法。

Method: 提出 ERAHBO 方法，采用异方差高斯过程对超参数配置下的回报均值和方差进行联合建模，并设计风险厌恶的采集函数以平衡高回报与低方差。同时引入自适应重采样机制，根据当前不确定性动态分配评估预算，替代传统固定预算策略，从而提升样本效率。

Result: 在多种 RL 算法和环境上的实验表明，ERAHBO 在风险厌恶回报方面普遍优于风险中性和风险厌恶的基线方法，尤其在样本效率上表现突出。

Conclusion: ERAHBO 通过异方差建模与自适应重采样的结合，实现了对 RL 超参数配置风险与回报的有效权衡，显著提升了超参数优化的样本效率，为随机性强的 RL 训练场景提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success across a wide range of complex tasks. However, RL outcomes can be highly stochastic, and both expected performance and variability often depend on hyperparameter (HP) configurations. We propose efficient and risk-averse heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization (ERAHBO), a Bayesian optimization method that models both the mean and variance of learning outcomes as functions of the HP configurations. ERAHBO aims to identify HP configurations that achieve high average return while reducing variability across training runs, and it improves the sample efficiency of the HP optimization via adaptive re-sampling rather than a fixed budget per HP. Empirical evaluations across diverse RL algorithms and environments demonstrate that ERAHBO generally outperforms both risk-neutral and risk-averse baselines, delivering improved sample efficiency for risk-averse returns.

</details>


### [243] [Mixture-of-experts for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26708)
*Florent Imbert,Eric Anquetil,Yann Soullard,Romain Tavenard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于混合专家（MOE）模型的数字笔在线手写轨迹重建方法，分别用触笔专家模型和悬停专家模型处理触纸与悬空轨迹，并通过引入新的公开基准数据集验证了该方法相较现有方案的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字笔在手写轨迹重建中，难以同时精细重建触纸轨迹和准确分析悬停轨迹以正确定位下一笔触位置。需要一个能够分别处理这两种不同模式、并在课堂写字学习等场景中实用的解决方案。

Method: 采用混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MOE）架构：第一个专家（touching expert model）专注于笔尖触纸时的轨迹重建；第二个专家（hovering expert model）专注于笔悬空时的轨迹分析。每个专家模型通过额外的上下文信息或特定样本进行增强学习。同时构建了一个新的公开基准数据集。

Result: 所提方法在重建精度上相较主要竞争对手实现了显著提升（significant enhancement）。

Conclusion: 基于MOE的触笔与悬停双专家模型能够有效提升在线手写轨迹重建的精细度和定位准确性，新公开的数据集也为该领域的后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: The use of digital pens for online handwriting trajectory reconstruction is a prevalent method for human-computer interaction. In this study, we focus on a digital pen equipped with sensors where we aim at reconstructing the online handwriting trajectory. This pen enables writing on any surface and preserving the digital trace of handwriting. This type of pen could be used as an aid to learning to write in classroom. In this paper, we propose a new approach learning to finely reconstruct the touching trajectories while precisely analyzing the hovering part in order to position the next touching trace correctly. This relies on a Mixture-Of-Experts (MOE) approach. The first expert is dedicated for the pencil touch, and is named touching expert model. The second one is dedicated for the hovering pen trajectory, and is named hovering expert model. We improve on the learning of each of these experts based on additional context or specific examples. In addition we introduce a novel public benchmark dataset, to enable future research and comparisons in the field of handwriting reconstruction. The results demonstrates a significant enhancement compared to its primary competitors.

</details>


### [244] [PowerAtlas: Towards Electricity-Computing Co-Scheduling for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26710)
*Kaiwen Jiang,Siya Xu,Ziyue Zhu,Chao Yang,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerAtlas 是一个基于LLM-agent的电力-计算协同调度框架，通过融合历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，生成同时满足电网运行规则和计算任务SLA的联合调度决策。论文与省级电网合作构建实验网络，并发布ECBench基准（2000个带最优解的调度实例），在11个LLM上验证了方法的可行性和成本收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载的快速增长使数据中心成为大规模、波动性强但时空灵活的电网负荷。通用LLM生成的调度方案在严格电网约束下往往不可行，会导致线路潮流越限和负荷未服务的问题，亟需一种能够协同电力与计算资源的调度框架。

Method: 提出PowerAtlas框架，这是一个LLM-agent系统，核心思路是将历史调度实例、电网领域知识和物理约束（如线路潮流限制）显式融入LLM的决策过程中。框架通过agent机制协调电力侧与计算侧的联合优化，确保输出方案既满足电网运行规则（避免潮流越限），又满足计算任务的服务等级协议（SLA）。此外，与中国省级电网合作构建了实验性电力-计算网络，并基于去标识化运行数据构建了ECBench基准数据集，包含2000个带有oracle最优解的调度实例。

Result: 在11个LLM上的实验表明，PowerAtlas在真实物理运行条件下表现有效，能够在三个开源骨干模型上持续实现可行调度和成本收益，相比通用LLM方案显著降低了线路潮流越限和未服务负载。

Conclusion: PowerAtlas证明了将领域知识和物理约束融入LLM-agent框架可有效解决电力-计算协同调度问题，为数据中心作为灵活电网负荷的协同优化提供了可行路径。公开的ECBench基准和代码也为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is turning data centers into large-scale, volatile, yet spatiotemporally flexible grid loads, creating an urgent need for coordinated electricity-computing scheduling. Under stringent grid constraints, schedules from general-purpose large language models (LLMs) are often infeasible, causing line-flow violations and unserved load. We present PowerAtlas, an LLM-agent framework for electricity-computing co-scheduling that integrates historical instances, domain knowledge, and physical constraints to produce joint decisions satisfying both grid operational rules and the service-level agreements (SLAs) of computing tasks. Working with a provincial power utility in China, we built an experimental electricity-computing network and validated the decision loop on real data-center data; from de-identified operational data we further constructed ECBench, a benchmark of 2,000 scheduling instances with oracle-optimal solutions. Experiments across eleven LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of PowerAtlas under realistic physical operating conditions, with consistent feasibility and cost gains across three open-weight backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/JAVA-Jiang/PowerAtlas.

</details>


### [245] [Domain adaptation for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26736)
*Florent Imbert,Romain Tavenard,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究配备运动传感器的数字笔，针对成人与儿童因书写速度与手势差异导致传感器信号不一致的问题，提出域自适应方法构建统一中间特征表示，以改善笔迹轨迹重建，并与从头训练和微调方法进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔在教育领域（尤其是儿童学习书写）具有重要潜力，但核心挑战在于：即使书写轨迹相似，成人与儿童因书写速度、运笔自信度等差异，导致惯性传感器捕获的信号存在显著差异。这使得基于成人数据训练的模型难以直接泛化到儿童场景，需要一种能够弥合该域差异的方法。

Method: 采用域自适应（Domain Adaptation）方法，通过构建统一的中间特征表示来弥合成人与儿童之间的信号域差异，从而实现轨迹重建模型的跨域迁移。实验中将该方法与两种基线策略对比：(1) 完全从头训练模型；(2) 在成人模型基础上进行微调（Fine-tuning）。

Result: 域自适应方法能够有效利用现有成人数据知识，构建出统一的特征表示，相较于从头训练和简单微调，在跨域（成人→儿童）轨迹重建任务上展现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: 域自适应方法在利用已有知识实现不同上下文（成人到儿童）的笔迹轨迹重建方面具有显著价值，有望推动基于运动传感器数字笔在教育场景中的实际应用。

Abstract: Digital pens are commonly used to write on digital devices, providing the handwriting trace and enhancing human-computer interation. This study focuses on a digital pen equipped with kinematic sensors, allowing users to write on any surface while simultaneously preserving a digital trajectory of handwriting. This technology holds significant potential as a valuable educational tool, particularly in classrooms where it can facilitate the process of learning to write. A major issue is based on the difference in captured signals between adults and children. For similar handwriting trace, we have large differences in sensor signals due to differences in speed and confidence in the handwriting gesture of children. To address this, we investigate a domain adaptation approach to build a unified intermediate feature representation aimed at facilitating the trajectory reconstruction. We demonstrate the interest of domain adaptation methods in leveraging existing knowledge for application in different contexts. Specifically, we compare our domain adaptation approach with two other methods: training the model from scratch and fine-tuning the model.

</details>


### [246] [CalTwin: Towards Calibrated, Shift-Robust Medical World Models via Fisher-Information Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26752)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CalTwin提出一种统一的轻量正则化方法，结合Fisher信息矩阵偏移惩罚与置信度错位惩罚，用于提升医学世界模型在协变量偏移下的多步预测可靠性，在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症挑战数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学世界模型在临床部署中面临两大失效模式：(1) 协变量偏移——训练数据分散在不同医院、扫描设备和时间中，导致潜在动力学预测器看到的特征分布在训练片段间及部署时不一致；(2) 置信度错位——多步预测在临床风险最高的区域往往过度自信。这两个问题需要统一解决。

Method: 提出CalTwin，将两个已有的惩罚项统一为单一轻量正则化目标：(1) 基于Fisher信息矩阵的偏移惩罚，源自作者先前关于碎片化协变量偏移修复的工作；(2) 置信度错位惩罚（CMP），源自作者先前关于校准视觉-语言分类的工作。两者共同作用于基于GRU的医学世界模型的潜在状态转移预测器。论文推导了联合目标函数，并分析了哪些证明步骤可直接从分类设置迁移、哪些需要适配。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症挑战数据集上，将两个医院系统作为顺序训练片段、未见过的系统作为分布外测试集。CalTwin使OOD下一步潜在状态MSE相比无惩罚基线降低9.1%（其中FIM惩罚单独贡献7.0%）；置信度错位惩罚带来的ECE降低真实但较小（CalTwin为0.7%，CMP单独为1.3%）。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移和置信度错位可通过统一的正则化框架同时缓解，FIM偏移惩罚对预测精度的贡献占主导，而置信度校准虽有改善但幅度有限。该框架为医学世界模型在碎片化数据环境中的可靠部署提供了一种轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Medical world models aim to learn a latent state of patient or organ physiology and a transition function that forecasts how that state evolves under interventions, supporting downstream tasks from imaging-based diagnosis to digital-twin treatment planning. Two failure modes threaten the reliability of such models in clinical deployment: (i)~\emph{covariate shift}, because training data are fragmented across hospitals, scanners, and time, so the feature distribution seen by the latent-dynamics predictor differs across fragments and from the distribution at deployment; and (ii)~\emph{confidence misalignment}, because multi-step forecasts are often overconfident exactly where clinical risk is highest. We argue that both problems admit a unified treatment via a single lightweight regularisation objective, \textbf{CalTwin}, which combines a Fisher-Information-based shift penalty adapted from our prior work on fragmented covariate-shift remediation~\cite{khan2025mitigating,khan2025causal} with a Confidence Misalignment Penalty adapted from our prior work on calibrated vision-language classification~\cite{khan2025confidence}, applied here to a GRU-based medical world model's latent transition predictor. We derive the combined objective, establish which proof steps transfer from the classification setting without modification and which require adaptation, and evaluate it on the PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge, treating the two hospital systems as sequential training fragments and the unseen system as an out-of-distribution test. CalTwin reduces OOD next-step latent-state MSE by 9.1\% relative to the no-penalty baseline (FIM penalty alone accounts for 7.0\%); the ECE reduction from the Confidence Misalignment Penalty is real but small (0.7\% for CalTwin, 1.3\% for CMP alone).

</details>


### [247] [Journey Operators for Structured Multi-Axis Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26775)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多轴结构数据的统一理论框架，揭示了RoPE等旋转位置编码方法的数学本质：当各轴变换可交换时，组合与移动是路径无关的。在此基础上设计了JoFormer模型，并在多模态任务上进行了初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据具有多轴结构（如文本的序列轴、图像的二维轴、视频的时空轴等）。沿同一轴，顺序至关重要；但跨独立轴时，组合与移动应与轴的顺序无关。现有方法（如RoPE及其多维变体）虽在实践中有效，但缺乏统一的理论框架来解释其数学本质和适用条件。

Method: 作者构建了一个多轴结构建模框架：每个数据项携带其内容及每个轴的小变换，连接两位置的路径定义了一个"旅程"（journey），旅程算子是沿该路径各轴变换的乘积。文章严格证明了：当各轴变换可交换时，跨轴组合与移动是路径无关的；并在环面框架对称性、cocycle、双线性和范数保持等假设下，证明成对评分规则必然取分块旋转形式，从而自然导出RoPE类方法。基于该理论，作者设计了JoFormer用于值聚合，并将其与注意力和状态空间模型建立联系。

Result: 理论结果表明：RoPE及其多维变体是该框架在各轴变换固定时的特例；当变换依赖数据时，模型获得内容自适应的位置归纳偏置。初步实验在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上表明，这些归纳偏置在实践中具有可观测的效果。

Conclusion: 多轴结构数据的核心数学条件是轴变换的可交换性，这保证了路径无关性。在此条件下，旋转位置编码形式是自然且必然的选择。该理论为设计和理解位置编码方法提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Many kinds of data have structure along one or more axes: words in a sentence, pixels in an image, nodes in a tree, frames in audio, or cells in a 3D volume. Along one axis, order matters: "the dog bit the man" is different from "the man bit the dog." Across independent axes, however, neither composition nor movement should depend on the order of axes: in an image, composing right then down should give the same result as composing down then right, and moving right then down should describe the same relative position as moving down then right.
  We develop a framework for modeling this kind of multi-axis structure. Each data item carries its content together with a small transformation for each axis. A path connecting two positions defines a journey; the journey operator is the product of per-axis transformations along that path, governing both how data composes along the path and how relative position is described. When the transformations are fixed, our framework recovers Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and its multi-dimensional variants. When they depend on the data, the model gains a content-adaptive positional inductive bias.
  We show exactly when these paths are well-defined: both composition and movement across axes are path-independent precisely when the axis transformations commute. We also prove that, under the stated toral-frame symmetry, cocycle, bilinearity, and norm-preservation assumptions, the resulting pairwise scoring rule must take the form of block-wise rotations, explaining why RoPE-like methods arise naturally.
  Finally, we use this theory to design JoFormer, a model for value aggregation, and relate it to attention and state-space models (SSMs).
  Initial experiments across vision, language, and length generalization suggest that these inductive biases can have observable consequences in
  practice.

</details>


### [248] [SkillRise: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Cross-Task Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26784)
*Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Zishan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Zhengzhou Cai,Kangning Zhang,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Ziang Ye,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiwen Liu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillRise 是一个统一的强化学习框架，让 LLM Agent 在跨任务学习中交替进行任务求解和技能文档的策划更新，通过解耦的信用分配机制分别优化当前任务表现和下游任务收益，在多个基准上取得最优 Pass@1 性能，并展现出跨任务技能复用和测试时扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM Agent 的强化学习将任务视为独立 episode，而现有技能学习方法要么局限于单一任务的重复尝试，要么使用多阶段流水线将技能提取、检索和执行耦合在一起，缺乏一个统一、简洁的跨任务技能学习框架。

Method: 提出 SkillRise 框架：(1) 将相关任务实例组织为难度逐步递增的序列；(2) 使用单一策略在任务求解与技能文档策划之间交替切换，技能文档直接传递给下一个任务；(3) 解耦的信用分配机制——当前任务结果监督求解行为，折扣的下游任务结果监督策划行为。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 三个基准上，SkillRise 的 Pass@1 性能均优于所有对比方法，相比最强基线提升 2.3–8.5 个百分点。其策划策略在单任务重复尝试场景中同样有效。测试时跨任务扩展分析表明：即使每个任务仅尝试一次，性能仍随相关任务序列增长而提升，验证了跨任务技能复用。同时大幅降低了多阶段技能学习流水线的运行时开销。

Conclusion: SkillRise 提供了一个简单高效的训练范式，使 LLM Agent 能够跨任务提取、精炼和复用可迁移技能，统一了任务求解与技能积累过程，在性能和效率上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model agents often encounter related yet distinct tasks that share reusable solution patterns. Yet standard agentic reinforcement learning treats tasks as independent episodes, while existing approaches to skill learning either focus on repeated attempts of one task or use pipelines with multiple stages that entangle extraction, retrieval, and execution. We introduce SkillRise, a unified reinforcement learning framework for learning skills across tasks. SkillRise organizes related instances into progressively challenging sequences and uses a single policy to alternate between task solving and curating an evolving skill document passed directly to the next task. Decoupled credit assignment across tasks supervises solving with the current task outcome and curation with discounted downstream outcomes. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show that SkillRise achieves the strongest Pass@1 performance among the compared methods, with gains over the strongest baseline ranging from 2.3 to 8.5 percentage points. Although trained across distinct tasks, its learned curation policy remains effective for repeated attempts on the same task. Further analysis reveals scaling at test time across tasks: performance improves with longer sequences of related tasks even when each task is attempted only once. This trend suggests that SkillRise reuses transferable skills across tasks rather than benefiting from repeated sampling of the same task. SkillRise further retains strong performance while substantially reducing the runtime overhead of skill learning pipelines with multiple stages. Together, these results provide a simple and efficient training paradigm for LLM agents to extract, refine, and reuse transferable skills across tasks.

</details>


### [249] [FedTopo: Relation-Level Topology Sharing for Model-Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26801)
*Zhaoyang Ma,Zhihao Wu,Xin Gao,Lipo Wang,Youfang Lin,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTopo 是一种联邦学习框架，将全局知识编码为类别关系拓扑（class relation topology），而非传统的参数、预测或原型。通过聚合各客户端上传的关系拓扑和类别统计，服务器以可靠性感知方式广播全局拓扑，指导本地训练。在多种异构骨干网络上，FedTopo 在三个数据集上一致优于现有基线，且通信开销低、无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中异构本地架构导致表示空间不对齐，现有方法（参数共享、知识蒸馏、原型共享）都依赖于绝对空间中对齐的知识传递，而异构骨干网络破坏了这种对齐，使共享知识不可靠并误导本地训练。因此需要一种不依赖绝对空间对齐的知识共享方式。

Method: 提出 FedTopo 框架：每个客户端基于本地原型构建类别关系拓扑，连同类别统计上传至服务器；服务器以可靠性感知方式聚合这些关系（降低弱支持关系的权重），生成全局关系拓扑并广播；全局拓扑通过强调拓扑相似的负类来指导本地训练，无需在绝对特征空间中对齐表示。

Result: 在三个数据集、八种异构骨干网络上的实验表明，FedTopo 在性能上持续优于参数共享、知识蒸馏和原型共享等基线方法，同时保持低通信开销且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: FedTopo 通过关系级知识编码有效解决了异构架构下的表示空间不对齐问题，以类关系拓扑替代绝对空间中的知识表示，为异构联邦学习提供了一种轻量、高效的新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning over decentralized data silos without centralizing raw data. However, heterogeneous local architectures often induce non-aligned representation spaces, making it difficult to transfer global knowledge across silos. Existing paradigms share this knowledge as model parameters, distilled predictions, or class prototypes, yet all encode it in an absolute space that must be aligned across clients. Heterogeneous backbones break this alignment, so the shared knowledge becomes unreliable and misleads local training. We propose FedTopo, a relation-level framework that encodes global knowledge as class relation topology, capturing how classes relate within each client rather than where they lie in feature space. Each client builds its relation topology from local prototypes and uploads it with class statistics. The server then aggregates these relations in a reliability-aware manner that down-weights weakly supported ones, and broadcasts the global topology to clients. The global topology guides local training by emphasizing topology-similar negative classes. Experiments on three datasets under eight heterogeneous backbones show that FedTopo consistently outperforms parameter-, distillation-, and prototype-sharing baselines, with low communication and no inference overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zhaoyang-Ma/FedTopo.

</details>


### [250] [Forecasting Trajectory-Level Safety Risks in Black-Box Multi-Turn Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26820)
*Shi Lin,Peng Qian,Dinghao Liu,Renjie Sun,Sifan Wu,Dezhang Kong,Chenpei Wang,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Recast，一种轨迹级安全风险预测框架，通过双尺度轨迹视图检索风险证据并建模风险演化的时序动态，在 7 个风险类别上以平均提前 2.41 轮预测了 88.3% 的未来安全故障，误报率仅为 12.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 安全防护主要依赖单轮点状违规检测，缺乏对多轮交互中恶意意图逐步分解、跨轮重构并最终导致安全失败的过程理解，无法实现风险的预判与主动预防。

Method: Recast 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双尺度轨迹视图，从短期对话进展和长期历史上下文中检索风险相关证据；(2) 组合式风险演化建模，捕捉当前风险配置及其时序动态；(3) 因果时序编码器，学习潜在风险演化模式并预测未来风险出现轮次的分布。

Result: 在 7 个风险类别上的实验表明，Recast 成功预测了 88.3% 的未来安全故障，平均提前量为 2.41 轮，误报率仅为 12.3%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级风险预测相比轮次级违规检测具有显著优势，能够在实际安全违规发生前识别新兴风险，为 LLM agent 的安全防护从被动响应转向主动预防提供了有效路径。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve from standalone assistants into autonomous agents, ensuring their safety requires shifting beyond pointwise risk assessment to understand how risks emerge and unfold over long-horizon trajectories. In multi-turn interactions, malicious intent can be decomposed across seemingly harmless turns and gradually reconstructed through interaction trajectories, eventually resulting in safety failures. Existing safeguards remain largely reactive, detecting manifested violations while lacking the ability to predict latent risk evolution and enable preemptive prevention. To address this limitation, we propose Recast, a safety risk forecasting framework that advances LLM safeguarding beyond turn-level violation detection to trajectory-level risk prediction. Recast first retrieves risk-relevant evidence from both short-term dialogue progression and long-term historical context via a dual-scale trajectory view. It then models compositional risk evolution by capturing the current risk configuration and its temporal dynamics. Finally, a causal temporal encoder learns latent risk evolution patterns and predicts the distribution of future risk emergence turns. Extensive experiments across 7 risk categories show that Recast predicts 88.3% of future safety failures with an average lead time of 2.41 turns, while maintaining a false alarm rate of 12.3%, showcasing the effectiveness of trajectory-level forecasting in identifying emerging risks before safety violations occur.

</details>


### [251] [Budget-Aware LLM Discovery via Cost-Calibrated Frontier Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26828)
*Yansen Zhang,Yilu Liu,Tianyu Liu,Jiamin Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Kai Xie,Xue Liu,Chen Ma,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CostAda，一种成本校准的自适应搜索控制器，通过引入“成本校准前沿效用”来在推理时搜索中平衡质量收益与token成本，在预算约束下显著提升搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应发现控制器仅基于分数进展分配信用，忽略了不同搜索动作（如提示长度、重试次数、引导调用）带来的差异化token成本。作者证明了当搜索前沿增多且成本分化时，忽略成本的信用分配会丧失几乎所有可达质量，因此需要一种在有限token预算下能权衡前沿进展与实现成本的控制器。

Method: 提出 CostAda 控制器，核心是“成本校准前沿效用”（cost-calibrated frontier utility），该效用将前沿进展相对于实际动作成本进行估值，并以剩余预算为条件。基于此信号，CostAda 控制局部探索强度、前沿分配以及预算化策略干预，使成本和剩余预算成为塑造搜索过程的核心变量，而非仅作为记账或停止规则。

Result: 在16个benchmark-backbone配对中的12对上，CostAda仅用基线一半的预算即达到最强基线的全预算质量；在GLM-5和GPT-5.4下的全部8个benchmark上，CostAda取得了最强的平均最终质量。

Conclusion: 将成本感知引入自适应搜索控制可以大幅提升推理时搜索的预算效率，CostAda证明了成本与剩余预算应作为搜索决策的核心信号，为实现更高效的科学与算法发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly support scientific and algorithmic discovery through inference-time search over evaluated candidates. Existing adaptive discovery controllers assign credit based only on score progress, even though prompt length, retries, and guidance calls cause search actions to incur different token costs. We prove that cost-blind credit can forfeit all but a vanishing fraction of attainable quality as frontiers multiply and costs diverge. Under a fixed search-side token budget, the controller must decide which frontier is improving and whether its gain justifies the realized cost before the budget is exhausted. We introduce \textbf{CostAda}, a cost-calibrated adaptive controller built around \emph{cost-calibrated frontier utility}. The utility values frontier progress relative to realized action cost and conditions that credit on the remaining budget. CostAda uses this signal to control local exploration intensity, frontier allocation, and budgeted tactic intervention. Cost and remaining budget therefore shape the search rather than serving only as accounting variables or a stopping rule. CostAda reaches the strongest baseline's full-budget quality with at most half the budget on twelve of sixteen benchmark--backbone pairs while achieving the strongest mean final quality on all eight benchmarks under GLM-5 and GPT-5.4.

</details>


### [252] [Kairos: Numerically Robust News Recommendation under Item Cold-Start via Cholesky-based LinUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26832)
*Finn Hertsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Project Kairos 提出一种面向区域新闻市场的推荐框架，通过 LinUCB 上下文在线学习解决物品冷启动问题，结合 Cholesky 分解的秩1更新保证数值稳定性，并利用 Matryoshka 表示学习优化推理延迟，最终实现 4.85 倍效率提升而不显著损失排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域市场中的新闻个性化推荐面临结构性数据稀缺：新闻存活时间短（TTL < 48小时）、文章池浅，导致协同过滤等深度学习模型因缺乏交互数据而失效（物品冷启动问题）。需要一个能在资源受限和数据稀疏条件下持续运行的高性能推荐方案。

Method: 1. 采用 LinUCB 上下文在线学习（contextual bandit）框架，在稀疏交互数据下进行探索-利用平衡的推荐决策；2. 将传统 Sherman-Morrison 求逆替换为 Cholesky 因子的直接秩1更新，确保协方差矩阵在病态数据场景下仍保持正定性；3. 集成 Matryoshka 表示学习（MRL），利用特征空间的语义冗余降低推理时的计算开销。

Result: 基于 Tagesschau API 的实证评估表明，Kairos 利用特征空间语义冗余实现了 4.85 倍的效率增益，且排序精度未受显著影响（without significantly compromising ranking precision）。

Conclusion: Kairos 为资源和数据受限环境下的高性能推荐系统提供了可行蓝图，证明了通过数值稳定的在线学习与表示学习协同设计，可以同时解决冷启动数据稀缺和推理延迟两大难题。

Abstract: Algorithmic news personalization in regional markets often fails because modern deep learning models require massive interaction data while real-world news has a short Time-to-Live (TTL < 48 h) and shallow article pools. This structural item cold-start deprives collaborative filtering of the data needed for robust modeling. This paper presents Project Kairos, a framework that bridges this data scarcity through a contextual online learning approach (LinUCB). To ensure numerical integrity for continuous operation, Kairos replaces error-prone Sherman-Morrison inversions with direct rank-1 updates of Cholesky factors. This preserves the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix even under ill-conditioned data scenarios. Simultaneously, Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) integration addresses inference latency. Empirical evaluations based on the Tagesschau API demonstrate that exploiting semantic redundancy in the feature space achieves a 4.85-fold efficiency gain without significantly compromising ranking precision. Kairos thus provides a blueprint for high-performance recommendation systems in resource- and data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [253] [Tight Generalization Bound for AdaBoost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26838)
*Mikael Møller Høgsgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了AdaBoost的泛化误差界为Θ(d ln(nγ²/d)/(nγ²) + ln(1/δ)/n)，其中γ为弱学习器保证的优势，d为弱假设类的VC维，n为样本量，δ为置信参数。主要贡献是给出了上界证明，下界来自先前工作。证明通过结合AdaBoost输出零经验γ/2-间隔损失的投票分类器这一已知事实，以及一个新的基于间隔的投票分类器泛化界来完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前AdaBoost的泛化误差界在样本复杂度上存在改进空间，作者旨在给出一个更紧致的上界，精确刻画AdaBoost的泛化性能，并使之与已知下界匹配，从而完全确定AdaBoost泛化误差的渐近行为。

Method: 作者首先利用AdaBoost输出投票分类器且该分类器具有零经验γ/2-间隔损失这一已知性质；其次，推导出一个新的基于间隔的投票分类器泛化界（margin-based generalization bound），该界将VC维、间隔优势γ、样本量n和置信参数δ有机结合起来，最终得到匹配上下界的紧致刻画。

Result: 得到AdaBoost泛化误差的紧致界：Θ(d ln(nγ²/d)/(nγ²) + ln(1/δ)/n)。上界为本文主要贡献，下界与先前工作一致，两者匹配表明该界在渐近意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: AdaBoost的泛化误差由弱假设类的VC维、弱学习器优势γ、样本量和置信参数共同决定。该紧致界从理论上完整解释了AdaBoost的泛化行为，并表明基于间隔的分析框架能够给出最优的泛化保证。

Abstract: In this paper we show that the generalization error of AdaBoost is $Θ\big(\tfrac{d\ln(nγ^{2}/d)}{nγ^2}+\tfrac{\ln(1/δ)}{n}\big)$, where $γ$ is the advantage guaranteed by the weak learner, $d$ is the VC-dimension of the class containing the weak hypotheses, $n$ is the sample size, and $δ$ is the confidence parameter. The contribution of this paper is the upper bound; the matching lower bound follows from prior work. The upper bound proof follows by combining the known fact that AdaBoost outputs a voting classifier whose voting function has zero empirical $γ/2$-margin loss with what is, to the best of our knowledge, a new margin-based generalization bound for voting classifiers.

</details>


### [254] [Thinking Under Uncertainty: Evidence Use and Information-Seeking in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26845)
*Hua-Dong Xiong,Xinyuan Yan,Ji-An Li,Jingming Xue,Marcelo G. Mattar,Robert C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了推理时思考（inference-time thinking）对大型语言模型决策行为的影响，发现思考主要改善了模型对当前证据的利用能力，而非促使其转向更主动的信息探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注推理时思考带来的整体性能提升，但未揭示模型内部行为机制：究竟是更有效地利用了已有证据，还是更积极地为未来决策寻求信息。该文旨在通过区分利用（exploitation）与探索（exploration）这两类行为，填补这一空白。

Method: 使用10个开源权重模型，在成对的地平线式双臂赌博机任务中，比较"思考"和"非思考"两种模式下的表现。通过认知模型分离出价值引导行动、不确定性无关的选择噪声，以及两种探索行为特征：类似UCB的对未知臂偏好和类似Thompson采样的随总不确定性增加的选择变异性。同时测量了思考长度和报告的置信度。

Result: 平均而言，思考增强了价值引导行动，降低了不确定性无关的选择噪声，但未产生UCB式探索，也未增强Thompson式探索。在信息不平衡条件下，思考长度更长；报告置信度对决策难度更敏感，且与所选任务证据关联更强。解码参数扫描（尤其是温度）虽改变了选择噪声和思考长度，但未能复现上述联合跨输出模式。

Conclusion: 在受控决策环境中，推理时思考改善了模型对当前证据的利用方式，但两个测量的探索指标均不支持模型转向更倾向于信息搜寻的策略。思考长度和置信度模式分别暗示了元认知控制和元认知监测过程的存在。

Abstract: Inference-time thinking improves the performance of large language models, but aggregate outcomes do not reveal whether models use available evidence more effectively or seek information that could improve future decisions. We distinguish these responses by measuring action preference, thinking length, and reported confidence under matched uncertainty. Ten open-weight models completed matched horizon-style two-armed bandit trials in thinking and non-thinking modes. A cognitive model separated value-guided action and uncertainty-independent choice noise from two behavioral signatures of exploration: a UCB-like preference for the less-known arm and Thompson-like choice variability that increases with total uncertainty. On average, thinking strengthened value-guided action and reduced uncertainty-independent choice noise, without producing UCB-like exploration or strengthening Thompson-like exploration. Outside action, the information-imbalanced history condition, which also displayed more observations than the matched balanced condition, was associated with greater thinking length. Reported confidence became more sensitive to decision difficulty and more strongly associated with chosen task evidence. We interpret these thinking-length and reported-confidence patterns as consistent with metacognitive control and metacognitive monitoring, respectively, without establishing either process. Decoder sweeps, especially temperature, altered choice noise and thinking length but did not reproduce the joint cross-output pattern. In this controlled decision setting, thinking improved how models acted on current evidence, while neither measured signature supported a shift toward a more information-seeking policy.

</details>


### [255] [TREA-Net: A Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for Dengue Incidence Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26854)
*Inesh Shukla,Madhurima Panja,Tanujit Chakraborty,Chittaranjan Hens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREA-Net 是一种可迁移的残差流行病学适应网络，通过将环境驱动的 SIR 模型投影与轻量级门控残差校正相结合，增强神经预测骨干网络，从而在历史数据稀缺的地区实现准确的 8 周登革热预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新建的登革热监测系统缺乏训练可靠神经预测模型所需的历史数据，而现有预训练时间序列模型虽提供零样本预测，但其跨领域训练无法捕捉当地的流行病学动态。因此，需要一种能在数据稀缺条件下实现高精度多周登革热预测的迁移学习方法。

Method: TREA-Net 将环境时间序列 SIR 模型的投影作为增广特征注入神经预测骨干网络，并学习一个轻量级门控残差校正模块，该模块可从数据丰富地区迁移至数据稀缺地区。其节点不变设计适配不同地点数量的监测系统，目标适应仅需学习两个全局参数。研究将知识从哥伦比亚和尼加拉瓜的长期监测数据迁移至墨西哥和马来西亚，进行 8 周预测，目标数据仅需 78 或 104 周。

Result: 在 5 种神经骨干网络和 10 种迁移设置中，TREA-Net 在 9/10 的设置中改善了对应骨干网络的表现，且提升具有统计显著性。与基础预测模型 TiRex 集成后，在所有目标数据集上实现了最低的平均绝对误差。共形预测在墨西哥数据上将 8 周预测区间宽度缩减了 29.6%，同时保持了经验覆盖率。

Conclusion: TREA-Net 是一种轻量级、可移植的早期预警框架，适用于监测数据有限的卫生机构，具有显著的实用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate multi-week dengue forecasting supports timely vector-control interventions, outbreak preparedness, and healthcare resource allocation. However, newly established surveillance systems often lack the historical data needed to train reliable neural forecasting models. Although pretrained time-series models offer promising zero-shot forecasts, their cross-domain training may not capture local epidemiological dynamics. We propose TREA-Net, a Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for dengue forecasting under limited data. TREA-Net augments neural forecasting backbones with projections from an Environmental Time-Series Susceptible-Infected-Recovered model and learns a lightweight gated residual correction transferable from data-rich to data-scarce regions. Its node-invariant design accommodates surveillance systems with different numbers of locations, while target adaptation requires learning only two global parameters. We transfer knowledge from long-running dengue surveillance in Colombia and Nicaragua to 8-week-ahead forecasting in Mexico and Malaysia using only 78 or 104 weeks of target data. Across five neural backbones and ten transfer settings, TREA-Net improves the corresponding backbone in 9 out of 10 settings, with statistically significant gains. When integrated with TiRex, a foundation model for forecasting, it achieves the lowest mean absolute error across all target datasets. Conformal prediction further maintains empirical coverage while reducing 8-week prediction-interval width by 29.6% in Mexico. These results demonstrate TREA-Net's potential as a lightweight and portable early-warning framework for health agencies with limited surveillance data.

</details>


### [256] [Amortized Moment Matching for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26860)
*Wenze Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出摊销矩匹配（Amortized Moment Matching），利用神经网络学习数据矩作为分布训练信号。通过多项式投影框架，n阶投影可识别n+1阶数据矩。基于此提出摊销Fréchet距离损失（AMFD），可动态学习条件矩，在高维数据上高效扩展。AMFD在ImageNet单步生成上显著超越FD基线，并在文生图任务中展现出强大的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Fréchet距离（FD）损失依赖显式的边缘矩计算，难以处理高维数据和条件分布。作者希望构建一种能够动态学习条件矩、无需显式矩阵运算即可扩展至高位数据的替代方案，同时提升生成模型的训练鲁棒性和指令遵循能力。

Method: 1. 将扩散去噪器通过多项式投影进行表示，建立矩摊销的通用框架，证明n阶投影可显式识别n+1阶数据矩。2. 从可处理的仿射情形推导出Amortized Fréchet Distance (AMFD)损失。3. 采用交替、无矩阵的优化管线动态学习条件矩，避免显式边缘矩计算。4. 将该损失应用于全局表征特征的后训练，以及条件感知的文生图生成任务。

Result: 1. AMFD的神经形式比精确统计匹配具有更鲁棒的训练动态，在FDr⁶指标上大幅超越FD基线。2. 在ImageNet上实现优越的单步生成性能。3. 前两阶矩仅在强语义空间中能够识别目标分布。4. 文生图任务中，AMFD的单步模型在GenEval基准上超越多步FLUX.2 4B教师模型，PickScore达到同等水平。

Conclusion: AMFD作为一种摊销矩匹配方法，通过神经网络动态学习条件矩，有效克服了传统FD损失在高维数据和条件生成中的局限。其在图像生成和文生图任务上的优异表现验证了该方法的实用性和扩展潜力，为生成模型的分布匹配提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We propose amortized moment matching, utilizing neural networks to learn data moments as distributional training signals. By casting diffusion denoisers through polynomial projections, we establish a general framework for moment amortization, revealing that an $n$-th degree projection explicitly identifies data moments up to order $n+1$. Derived from the tractable affine case, we instantiate the Amortized Fréchet Distance (AMFD) loss. Unlike FD-loss which relies on explicit marginal moment calculations, AMFD is able to dynamically learn conditional moments via an alternating, matrix-free optimization pipeline that effortlessly scales to high-dimensional data. When operating on global representation features, AMFD serves as a powerful post-training objective; empirically, its neural formulation yields more robust training dynamics than exact statistical matching, substantially surpassing the FD baseline on the FDr$^6$ metric and achieving superior one-step generation on ImageNet. Furthermore, it unlocks direct exploration within native generative spaces, suggesting that the first two moments can identify target distributions only in spaces with strong semantics. Finally, when scaled to text-to-image generation, the condition-aware nature of AMFD unlocks massive gains in instruction-following capabilities, enabling our one-step models to outperform their multi-step FLUX.2 [klein] 4B teachers on the GenEval benchmark while achieving on-par performance on PickScore. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/poppuppy/amfd.

</details>


### [257] [ReCo: Reweighting GRPO Against Distributional Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26862)
*Junoh Park,Junseo Hwang,Wonguk Cho,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO 在训练中会集中于基础模型已有的高概率响应，导致推理路径覆盖度下降，损害大 k 下的 Pass@k。本文提出 ReCo 方法，从响应层和 token 层两个层面重新加权，缓解该问题，在多个数学推理基准上提升了大 k 下的 Pass@k 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 成为语言模型后训练的标准强化学习方法，但近期研究发现它会削弱基础模型的推理能力，尤其在 k 较大时 Pass@k 表现不佳，说明推理路径覆盖度下降。作者旨在找出这一退化的根本原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者首先分析了 GRPO 退化的两个机制：(1) 响应层面，高概率响应因在 rollout group 中重复出现而主导组梯度；(2) token 层面，GRPO 的重要性比率缩放梯度，进一步强化当前策略下变得更可能的 token。基于此提出 ReCo 方法：响应贡献按其 rollout group 内的期望出现次数进行归一化；token 级重要性比率替换为基于方差的比率，对尚有替代 token 可选的非饱和决策点给予更大的更新尺度。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上的五个数学推理基准测试中，ReCo 在较大 k 值下显著提升了 Pass@k，在小 k 值下与 GRPO 表现相当。

Conclusion: GRPO 的推理覆盖度退化源于响应层和 token 层的双重集中效应，ReCo 通过响应归一化和方差驱动的 token 级重加权有效缓解了该问题，为大 k 推理场景提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a standard reinforcement learning method for post-training language models. Recent work shows that GRPO can reduce the base model's reasoning capacity and underperform it in Pass@k when k is large, indicating reduced coverage of reasoning paths. We find that this reduction is associated with GRPO concentrating on responses that the base model already generates with high probability. We trace this concentration to two mechanisms in the GRPO update. At the response level, high-probability responses dominate the group gradient through repeated occurrence. At the token level, GRPO's importance ratio scales gradients, further reinforcing tokens that become more likely under the current policy. We propose ReCo, a reweighting method that addresses both effects. Response contributions are normalized by their expected occurrence within the rollout group, and the token-level importance ratio is replaced with a variance-based ratio that gives larger update scale to non-saturated decision points where alternative token choices remain plausible. Across Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ReCo improves Pass@k for large values of k and is comparable to GRPO for small values of k.

</details>


### [258] [Actions Have Consequences: Detecting Outcome Performativity using Intervention Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26908)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPAB（Outcome Performativity A/B Detection）的方法，通过预测干预来检测结果执行性（Outcome Performativity），即预测本身因果性地影响其所预测结果的现象。该方法通过比较不同预测组产生的结果分布差异来判断执行性是否存在，并推导了不同假设类下的样本复杂度边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安宁疗护、信用分配和推荐系统等许多领域中，预测可能因果性地影响其所预测的结果，这种现象被称为"结果执行性"。检测这一现象的存在对于理解预测系统的实际影响至关重要，但此前缺乏系统化的检测方法。

Method: 提出OPAB方法：通过实施预测干预（即分配不同的预测组），评估各组产生的结果分布之间的差异性。若差异显著，则判定存在结果执行性。作者在不同的结果执行性假设类下推导了样本复杂度边界，并通过实证验证这些理论边界。

Result: 实证结果表明，OPAB在多种场景下能够有效检测结果执行性；同时揭示了"不可区分区域"的存在——当分配的干预样本数量不足时，无法检测出结果执行性。这对样本稀缺、成本高昂或伦理受限的实际场景具有重要启示。在Open Bandits数据集上的案例研究验证了OPAB的有效性。

Conclusion: OPAB为检测结果执行性提供了一种可行的形式化方法，其理论分析和实证结果为实践中的可检测性边界提供了指导。未来可在更广泛的领域和假设类下进一步拓展该方法。

Abstract: In many domains such as Palliative Care, Credit Assignment and Recommender Systems, predictions may causally influence the outcomes they predict. This phenomena is known as Outcome Performativity. This paper formalises an approach for detecting Outcome Performativity using prediction intervention called Outcome Performativity A/B Detection (OPAB). OPAB enables the detection of Outcome Performativity by assessing the dissimilarity in outcome distributions produced by different predictions groups (interventions). If that dissimilarity is significant, Outcome Performativity is detected. We derive sample complexity bounds for OPAB under various Outcome Performative assumption classes which we empirically validate. Results show that detecting Outcome Performativity using OPAB is achievable in numerous cases. Results also show the presence of regions of indistinguishability which describe settings where the allotted number of interventions are insufficient for detecting Outcome Performativity. The results of which have broader practical implications for the detectability of Outcome Performativity in settings where samples are scarce, cost-prohibitive or potentially unethical to obtain. The paper concludes with a case study on the efficacy of OPAB on the Open Bandits dataset, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [259] [Two Calls Beat Five Agents: Evaluating Multi-Agent Pipelines Against Self-Refinement for Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26922)
*Ashish Prajapati,Om Mohite*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在本地7B模型(Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct)上部署了一个五角色的多智能体系统Parishad，在GSM8K和HumanEval上测试。发现JSON格式导致错误累积使准确率骤降，而纯文本格式恢复性能；简单的两次调用自优化策略反而以更低token消耗超越复杂多智能体架构。结果表明通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度更能决定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM流水线系统主要在大型商业模型上评测，缺乏在本地小模型上的系统研究。作者试图揭示多智能体架构在资源受限的7B模型部署中的真实有效性，以及通信格式、实现细节等实际因素对性能的影响。

Method: 构建Parishad——一个包含五个角色的结构化多智能体系统，部署于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct本地模型。在GSM8K(500题)和HumanEval(164题)两个数据集上，与直接提示(direct prompting)和两次调用自优化(two-call self-refinement)进行对比。测试了JSON与纯文本两种数据格式，并设计了任务感知的门控机制(V2)来改进HumanEval上的自优化策略。

Result: GSM8K上：直接提示准确率75.0%，多智能体JSON格式降至45.0%（因错误累积），纯文本格式恢复至82.0%；两次自优化V1达86.2%且token用量降低7.4倍。HumanEval上：直接准确率96.3%，V1主动破坏性能降至66.5%，任务感知门控V2恢复至95.1%。

Conclusion: 通信格式和实现细节对结果的塑造作用超过架构复杂度本身；针对本地7B模型部署，更简单的方法能够匹配甚至超越复杂的多智能体流水线。任务感知的门控机制可有效防止简单自优化策略对已高准确率任务的破坏。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipeline systems break down the task among multiple roles for better reasoning, but are benchmarked mainly with large-scale commercial models. In this study, we investigate Parishad, a structured multi-agent system involving five roles, by deploying it on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a local model, on two datasets: GSM8K (500 questions) and HumanEval (164 questions), compared with prompting directly and two-call self-refinement. The multi-agent system drops GSM8K accuracy from 75.0\% to 45.0\% with JSON data format due to the error accumulation problem. With plaintext format, the accuracy is restored to 82.0\%. A two-call self-refinement strategy (V1) can achieve 86.2\% accuracy on GSM8K, with 7.4$\times$ lower token usage. However, the same V1 implementation on HumanEval---where direct accuracy is already 96.3\%---actively destroys performance (66.5\%). A task-aware gated redesign (V2) applied to HumanEval preserves accuracy at 95.1\%. Our results demonstrate that communication format and implementation details determine outcomes more than architectural complexity, and that simpler approaches match or outperform multi-agent pipelines for local 7B model deployment. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [260] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGReg在单任务世界模型学习中表现良好，但在多任务场景下会导致任务间隐空间簇的可分性被压缩。本文提出将SIGReg应用于时间中心化的残差而非隐变量边缘分布，既保留防止坍缩的效果，又避免破坏多任务隐空间结构。在LIBERO基准上，该方法将平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIGReg在单任务中通过将隐变量边缘分布正则化为各向同性高斯来防止表示坍缩，但这一方法在扩展到多任务训练时会导致下游行为克隆性能显著下降。本文旨在揭示这一失效的根本原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者分析了SIGReg在多任务场景下的失效机制：边缘高斯化会压缩任务依赖的隐空间簇之间的分离度，导致跨任务和跨状态的表示混叠。为此，提出将SIGReg应用于时间中心化的残差（temporally centered residuals），而非原始的隐变量边缘分布。该替代目标不对簇中心之间的分离施加直接正则化压力，去除了对整个隐空间服从单一各向同性高斯的要求，同时保留了SIGReg的抗坍缩效果。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上：长序列套件（long-horizon suite）的下游成功率提升1.7倍；四个套件的平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。在无外部预训练的条件下，略微优于从头训练的Diffusion Policy，并接近大规模预训练策略基线的性能。

Conclusion: 边缘高斯先验与多任务隐空间结构之间存在结构性不兼容。将SIGReg应用于残差而非边缘分布，是一种简单且有效的路径，可实现稳定且可扩展的端到端多任务世界模型学习。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [261] [Surrogate assisted diversity estimation in neural ensemble search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26940)
*Alexandr Udeneev,Petr Babkin,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种双目标代理引导的神经集成搜索方法，使用两个独立的代理模型分别估计候选架构的预测准确性和多样性潜力，从而高效地在联合优化的搜索空间中识别出既独立强大又集体多样化的架构集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经架构搜索(NAS)已很昂贵，将其扩展到神经集成搜索(NES)时，需要联合优化个体架构及其集成组合，导致搜索空间指数增长、计算上不可行。现有方法难以在可承受的计算成本下同时兼顾个体模型质量和模型间多样性。

Method: 将候选架构表示为有向无环图(DAG)；独立训练两个代理模型——一个估计预测准确性，另一个估计多样性潜力；将两者的估计值结合，引导NES框架高效搜索；在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上验证。

Result: 最终集成在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上与Deep Ensembles和Random Search等标准基线相比，达到了具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 双目标代理引导的集成搜索策略有效解决了NES中搜索空间指数爆炸的问题，能够高效找到准确性与多样性兼顾的架构集合，验证了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Ensembles are a standard way to improve the performance and robustness of deep neural networks, but their effectiveness crucially depends on both the quality and the diversity of individual models. Most neural architecture search (NAS) methods are computationally expensive. Extending them to neural ensemble search (NES), which requires joint optimization of individual architectures and their ensemble composition, leads to an exponential growth of the search space and makes the problem computationally intractable. To address this, we introduce a dual-objective surrogate-guided ensemble search: candidate architectures are represented as directed acyclic graphs, and two surrogate models are trained independently to estimate predictive accuracy and diversity potential. Their combined estimates guide an NES framework that efficiently identifies architectures that are both individually strong and collectively diverse. Our final ensemble achieves competitive or superior performance compared to standard baselines such as Deep Ensembles and Random Search on FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [262] [Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Unified Linear-Probing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26993)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了24种冻结的预训练视觉编码器在人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，发现冻结的基础模型表征可通过线性分类器实现较强的数据集内性能，但跨数据集迁移能力不可靠，显式域适应仍是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸PAD面临跨数据集域偏移的挑战，且大规模标注数据稀缺。研究者希望探明通用视觉基础模型的预训练表征中是否已编码了PAD相关信息，以及这些信息能否通过极简的任务特定训练被有效提取。

Method: 在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上，采用统一线性探测协议评估24种冻结编码器，包括自监督视觉Transformer、视觉-语言编码器和有监督CNN。仅训练轻量线性分类头，保持骨干网络冻结，以隔离预训练表征中已有的PAD信息。

Result: 冻结基础模型表征配合线性分类器可获得较强的数据集内PAD性能，但跨数据集迁移不可靠。模型规模在部分模型族内有益，但效应非单调，受架构和预训练方式强烈中介。InternViT-6B实现最低平均数据集内错误率，CLIP ViT-B/32在跨数据集迁移-计算权衡上表现最优。

Conclusion: 预训练表征确实包含PAD相关信息，但仅靠冻结表征不足以应对域偏移，显式域适应策略仍然是实现鲁棒跨数据集人脸PAD的必要手段。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) remains challenging under cross-dataset evaluation, where domain shift degrades models trained on a single dataset. The scarcity of large-scale labeled data motivates adapting pretrained vision models rather than training task-specific architectures from scratch, raising a fundamental question: do general-purpose vision foundation models encode PAD-relevant information accessible with minimal task-specific training? To investigate, we systematically evaluate 24 frozen encoders, including self-supervised vision transformers, vision-language encoders, and supervised CNNs, using a unified linear-probing protocol on the MCIO benchmark (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU). The backbone remains fixed, and only a lightweight linear head is trained to isolate the PAD information already present in the pretrained representation. %We report intra- and cross-dataset performance, along with accuracy-compute trade-offs, relative to two specialist PAD baselines. Results show that frozen foundation-model representations can support strong intra-dataset PAD performance with only a linear classifier, but this performance does not reliably transfer across datasets. Model scale is beneficial within several families, although the effect is not monotonic and is strongly mediated by architecture and pretraining. InternViT-6B achieves the lowest mean intra-dataset error, whereas CLIP ViT-B/32 offers the most favorable cross-dataset transfer-compute trade-off among the evaluated probes. These findings suggest that while pretrained representations contain PAD-relevant information, explicit adaptation remains necessary to address domain shift.

</details>


### [263] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过可控干预实验，系统性地揭示了潜世界模型中"哪些物理量进入隐表征"由两个组织机制决定：输入模态限制了可知范围，预测目标决定了保留内容。刚度仅在预测触觉时进入潜变量，而阻力(drag)虽可恢复但任何确定性预测目标均无法习得——因其读出为"慢速比率型"。在RH20T真实机器人数据上的大规模实验验证了这两个机制的跨场景普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型的核心前提是"预测未来迫使表征内化环境物理"，但训练后的潜变量究竟包含哪些物理量、又由什么因素决定，此前缺乏系统的因果性回答。现有研究多依赖相关性分析，无法区分"物理量不可恢复"与"目标函数不要求习得"，导致归因不清。

Method: 提出"证书门控协议"(certificate-gated protocol)：先在原始观测上为每个物理参数颁发"可恢复性证书"，再测量其是否进入潜变量。这样若出现零结果，可归因于目标函数而非环境限制。在POKEWORLD环境中设计视觉相同但质量、阻力、接触刚度各异的物体，通过因子化操控输入模态（视觉/触觉）和预测目标（单步/多步、是否包含触觉），绘制"可辨识性图谱"。进一步在RH20T数据集上，对两个机器人、4258条episode进行输入-目标的因子化缩放曲线实验，验证机制普适性。

Result: 发现两个组织机制与一个前沿边界：(1)输入限制可知范围；(2)预测目标决定保留内容。刚度仅在预测触觉时进入潜变量(R²=0.50)，而相同信号仅作为输入融合时R²=-0.02。单步预测下纯视觉潜变量甚至丢弃完全可见的物体状态。阻力构成"前沿边界"：可恢复性证书达0.89，但在所有确定性预测目标下R²平台期仅约0.13，而监督探针在相同主干网络可达0.45。原因在于阻力读出是"慢速比率型"。RH20T实验复现两种机制：缺输入信息或缺预测压力的臂在五倍数据范围内表现平坦，仅全模态预测力的目标超越持久性基线，且增益随规模增长。

Conclusion: 目标函数的结构决定了潜变量获取哪些物理参数，而增加数据仅能改善其已获取的参数——即"目标结构决定上限，数据仅优化已有能力"。这一发现对设计具身智能中的世界模型和表征学习具有直接的指导意义。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [264] [TreeCCA: Canonical Correlation Analysis via Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27027)
*James Chapman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeCCA 首次将梯度提升树集成为 CCA 编码器，通过 Eckart-Young 损失实现端到端训练，兼具非线性精度与原生可解释性，在合成和真实多视图数据集上匹配或超越 Deep CCA 和线性 CCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 CCA 长期依赖线性或神经编码器，梯度提升树虽在表格数据中占主导地位，却从未被用作 CCA 编码器。研究者希望利用 GBT 的即插即用可靠性（无需架构设计、超参数熟悉、默认性能强）来填补空白，同时获得神经方法缺乏的原生可解释性。

Method: 提出 TreeCCA：将梯度提升树集成作为 CCA 编码器端到端训练。核心技术是 Eckart-Young (EY) 损失，它为每个样本提供闭式梯度，可直接作为自定义目标嵌入标准 GBT 库（XGBoost、LightGBM）。TreeCCA 中每次树分裂仅选择一个特征，因此增益重要性可零成本揭示哪些输入驱动跨视图相关性，实现原生可解释性。还扩展到多视图场景 TreeMCCA。

Result: 在合成基准上，TreeCCA 匹敌或超越 Deep CCA（Signed Power: 2.61 vs 2.43；Hermite: 2.93 vs 2.89）；在零线性协方差的稀疏基准上，TreeCCA 在 p=50 时实现 Precision@S=1.00 完全恢复真实支持，而 PMD 无法发现信号。在 UCI HAR 传感器融合基准上，TreeCCA 以 Deep CCA 五分之一的成本达到相当精度，且 XGBoost 增益重要性直接验证了物理动机假设。在五个流行表格多视图数据集上，TreeMCCA 在非线性相关性提取和下游分类精度方面一致匹配或超过线性 CCA。

Conclusion: TreeCCA 成功将 GBT 的优势引入 CCA 领域，实现了非线性精度与原生可解释性的结合，在多个基准上表现出色，为表格多视图学习提供了一种可靠、高效且可解释的新方法。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees dominate tabular machine learning, yet canonical correlation analysis has always relied on linear or neural encoders. We propose \textbf{TreeCCA}, the first method to train gradient-boosted tree ensembles end-to-end as CCA encoders, inheriting their plug-and-play reliability: no architecture design, familiar hyperparameters, and strong performance with defaults. The technical enabler is the Eckart-Young (EY) loss, which supplies closed-form per-sample gradients that slot directly into any standard GBT library (XGBoost, LightGBM) as a custom objective.
  TreeCCA is the first CCA method to combine nonlinear accuracy with native interpretability: every tree split selects one feature, so gain importances reveal which inputs drive cross-view correlation at no extra cost. We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [265] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从实际部署角度系统审计了稀疏/压缩模型替代稠密模型时的行为兼容性问题，发现即使准确率匹配，稀疏候选模型在校准、OOD响应、类别可靠性、表示及下游决策等多个维度上仍与原始稠密模型存在显著偏差，彩票票据替换会导致7%-10%的接受/拒绝决策变更，揭示了仅凭干净准确率认证的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据对模型行为的影响结论矛盾（有益或有害均有报告），且先前工作均未考虑实际部署条件——即下游决策逻辑已针对现有模型固定。因此，作者从生产替换的实际视角出发，研究准确率匹配的稀疏模型能否在不重新配置下游决策逻辑的情况下直接替代现有稠密模型。

Method: 作者构建了一个涵盖多维度部署相关行为的审计协议面板，包括校准（calibration）、分布外（OOD）响应、类别级可靠性、表征（representations）以及下游策略决策。通过定义"行为兼容性距离"（排除干净准确率偏差），在广泛的实验设置中系统比较稀疏候选模型与稠密参考模型的行为差异；特别在固定阈值策略诊断的小间隔场景下，量化彩票票据替换对接受/拒绝决策的扰动（churn）。此外，作者从理论上解释了路由结果：即使是精确的逐点top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变更，置信度在决策边界附近的微小偏移即可产生一阶路由扰动。

Result: 稀疏候选模型反复恢复了稠密模型的准确率，但在行为上仍保持显著差异；在多个研究组匹配的设置中，彩票票据（LT）还表现出更低的腐败准确率（corruption accuracy）。在固定阈值策略诊断的小间隔设置下，彩票票据替换导致7%到10%的接受/拒绝决策发生变更，这种扰动恰恰产生了直接替换试图避免的负担——即必须重新配置和重新验证下游决策逻辑。

Conclusion: 研究确立了干净准确率认证的局限性：与固定现有模型建立兼容性，不同于将扰动单独归因于稀疏性，也不同于将所有测量偏差视为有害。理论上证明了即使精确的逐点top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变更，决策边界附近的小置信度偏移即可引发一阶路由扰动。稀疏模型作为"即插即用"替代品的假设在实践中并不成立。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [266] [CoCaRS: Correlation Calibration-Based Redundancy Suppression for Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27054)
*Fengming Yu,Haiwei Pan,Kejia Zhang,Chunling Chen,Jian Guan,Baoying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCaRS 提出一种基于相关性校准的冗余抑制方法，通过混淆证据估计（CEE）和强度分配控制（SAC）校准特征去相关过程，并利用自适应系数调节（ACR）降低对超参的敏感性，从而在异构知识蒸馏中更好地保留结构信息、提升蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构知识蒸馏中，师生模型架构差异导致表示鸿沟；现有冗余抑制方法通过统一去相关减少特征冗余，但可能破坏有用的结构信息，且固定系数使方法对师生配对和训练阶段高度敏感。

Method: 提出 CoCaRS，包含三个核心组件：(1) 混淆证据估计（CEE），捕获可靠语义关系用于相关性估计；(2) 强度分配控制（SAC），在去相关过程中保留判别性结构；(3) 自适应系数调节（ACR），根据冗余抑制目标的相对损失尺度动态调节其贡献。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 和 ImageNet-1K 上的广泛实验表明，CoCaRS 有效提升了异构知识蒸馏性能，并显著降低了方法对系数设置的敏感性。

Conclusion: CoCaRS 通过校准去相关和自适应系数调节，解决了异构 KD 中冗余抑制方法的两大缺陷，在多个基准上验证了其优越性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables a compact student model to learn from a powerful teacher and has become an effective paradigm for model compression. The emergence of diverse model architectures has extended KD from homogeneous to heterogeneous settings.
